It’s like I start with the answer, but then it slips sideways. Someone asks about a ring repair, and I’m suddenly on invoices, or soil for the garden, or mortgage papers. I don’t mean to… I just lose the shape of what I was trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel that your thoughts don’t follow a clear path or that you start talking about one thing and end up somewhere completely different without meaning to?\n\nPatient: Yes… that happens. I’ll start answering something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and then I’m talking about invoices, or garden soil, or the mortgage papers. I don’t really mean to go there. I just notice people kind of… stop following me.\n\nDoctor: When you're speaking and your thoughts seem to go in different directions, do your words and sentences still make sense to you, even if others might not follow them?\n\nPatient: To me they do, mostly. Like, I know why invoices connect to the repair, and then somehow garden soil or the mortgage papers come in… it feels like there’s a reason in my head. But I can tell other people don’t see the path, and they start looking lost.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being put into your mind by someone else, or that your thoughts are being taken out of your mind or broadcast to others?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like… thoughts being put in or broadcast, no. It’s more… at the shop. Some customers ask too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re checking whether I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that simple to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear?\n\nPatient: Sometimes. And once in a while I smell burning metal even when the torches are off. It feels real at the time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear, even when you’re not in the shop or in quiet places?\n\nPatient: Mostly it’s at the shop, when it’s quiet. At work, someone asks when a ring repair will be ready, and I start talking about invoices, then somehow garden soil, mortgage papers… and I can tell they’re not following me. It’s embarrassing.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about how your thoughts move from one idea to another when you're trying to explain something? Do they feel connected or do they jump around in a way that's hard to follow?\n\nPatient: They feel connected when I’m saying it… at least at first. But then I’ll answer something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and somehow I’m talking about invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. I can see people’s faces change, like they’re losing track of me. I guess it jumps around more than I realize.\n\nDoctor: When you're trying to explain something, do you ever feel like your thoughts are slipping away or that you can't organize your ideas the way you used to?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. It’s like I start with the answer, but then it slips sideways. Someone asks about a ring repair, and I’m suddenly on invoices, or soil for the garden, or mortgage papers. I don’t mean to… I just lose the shape of what I was trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel that your thoughts don’t follow a clear path or that you start talking about one thing and end up somewhere completely different without meaning to?\n\nPatient: Yes… that happens. I’ll start answering something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and then I’m talking about invoices, or garden soil, or the mortgage papers. I don’t really mean to go there. I just notice people kind of… stop following me.\n\nDoctor: When you're speaking and your thoughts seem to go in different directions, do your words and sentences still make sense to you, even if others might not follow them?\n\nPatient: To me they do, mostly. Like, I know why invoices connect to the repair, and then somehow garden soil or the mortgage papers come in… it feels like there’s a reason in my head. But I can tell other people don’t see the path, and they start looking lost.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being put into your mind by someone else, or that your thoughts are being taken out of your mind or broadcast to others?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like… thoughts being put in or broadcast, no. But I do get convinced sometimes that certain customers are coming in to check on me—like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask too much about the new showroom, even though my husband says they’re just being friendly.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel that people are talking about you, watching you, or acting in a way that’s meant to send you a message, even if others don’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Sometimes, yes. I know it sounds… a little odd, but I get convinced certain customers are coming in to check on me, like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask so many questions about the new showroom. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you—like things on TV, in the news, or conversations—were directed at you or had a special meaning just for you?\n\nPatient: Not TV or the news, really. It’s more… at the shop. Some customers ask too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re checking whether I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that simple to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear?\n\nPatient: Sometimes. When the shop is really quiet, I’ve heard… like a woman muttering my name from the polishing room. I’ll go check, and no one’s there. And once in a while I smell burning metal even when the torches are off. It feels real at the time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear, even when you’re not in the shop or in quiet places?\n\nPatient: Mostly it’s at the shop, when it’s quiet. I can’t really think of it happening much outside of there. Like I said, I’ll hear a woman mutter my name from the polishing room, or smell burning metal when the torches are off. It feels real enough that I go check.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel like your thoughts are completely out of order, or that nothing makes sense in the way it used to?\n\nPatient: Yes… sometimes. That’s part of why I came in. Someone will ask me something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and I’ll start answering but then I’m talking about invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. I can see their face change, like they’re not following me… and I’m not really following myself either.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or being broadcast so that others can hear them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about thoughts being put in or broadcast, exactly. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or that they are being broadcast so others can hear them?\n\nPatient: Not really like someone putting thoughts in my head or hearing them out loud. It’s more… I get convinced people are watching for things. Like some customers asking too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re really there to see if I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but… I don’t know. It doesn’t feel that way.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I don’t mean to… I just lose the shape of what I was trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel that your thoughts don’t follow a clear path or that you start talking about one thing and end up somewhere completely different without meaning to?\n\nPatient: Yes… that happens. I’ll start answering something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and then I’m talking about invoices, or garden soil, or the mortgage papers. I don’t really mean to go there. I just notice people kind of… stop following me.\n\nDoctor: When you're speaking and your thoughts seem to go in different directions, do your words and sentences still make sense to you, even if others might not follow them?\n\nPatient: To me they do, mostly. Like, I know why invoices connect to the repair, and then somehow garden soil or the mortgage papers come in… it feels like there’s a reason in my head. But I can tell other people don’t see the path, and they start looking lost.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being put into your mind by someone else, or that your thoughts are being taken out of your mind or broadcast to others?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like… thoughts being put in or broadcast, no. But I do get convinced sometimes that certain customers are coming in to check on me—like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask too much about the new showroom, even though my husband says they’re just being friendly.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel that people are talking about you, watching you, or acting in a way that’s meant to send you a message, even if others don’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Sometimes, yes. I know it sounds… a little odd, but I get convinced certain customers are coming in to check on me, like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask so many questions about the new showroom. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you—like things on TV, in the news, or conversations—were directed at you or had a special meaning just for you?\n\nPatient: Not TV or the news, really. It’s more… at the shop. Some customers ask too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re checking whether I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that simple to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear?\n\nPatient: Sometimes. When the shop is really quiet, I’ve heard… like a woman muttering my name from the polishing room. I’ll go check, and no one’s there. And once in a while I smell burning metal even when the torches are off. It feels real at the time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear, even when you’re not in the shop or in quiet places?\n\nPatient: Mostly it’s at the shop, when it’s quiet. I can’t really think of it happening much outside of there. Like I said, I’ll hear a woman mutter my name from the polishing room, or smell burning metal when the torches are off. It feels real enough that I go check.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel like your thoughts are completely out of order, or that nothing makes sense in the way it used to?\n\nPatient: Yes… sometimes. That’s part of why I came in. Someone will ask me something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and I’ll start answering but then I’m talking about invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. I can see their face change, like they’re not following me… and I’m not really following myself either.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or being broadcast so that others can hear them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about thoughts being put in or broadcast, exactly. But… I have gotten pretty sure some customers are coming in just to see if I’m going to miss payments. They ask a lot about the new showroom, too many questions. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or that they are being broadcast so others can hear them?\n\nPatient: Not really like someone putting thoughts in my head or hearing them out loud. It’s more… I get convinced people are watching for things. Like some customers asking too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re really there to see if I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but… I don’t know. It doesn’t feel that way.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel like your thoughts are completely out of order or that you can’t make sense of your own ideas, even when you're not talking to anyone else?\n\nPatient: Yes… even when I’m by myself, it can feel tangled. Like I’ll be thinking about something simple at the shop, a ring repair maybe, and then suddenly I’m on invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. It’s hard to make the thread come back.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I don’t mean to… I just lose the shape of what I was trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel that your thoughts don’t follow a clear path or that you start talking about one thing and end up somewhere completely different without meaning to?\n\nPatient: Yes… that happens. I’ll start answering something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and then I’m talking about invoices, or garden soil, or the mortgage papers. I don’t really mean to go there. I just notice people kind of… stop following me.\n\nDoctor: When you're speaking and your thoughts seem to go in different directions, do your words and sentences still make sense to you, even if others might not follow them?\n\nPatient: To me they do, mostly. Like, I know why invoices connect to the repair, and then somehow garden soil or the mortgage papers come in… it feels like there’s a reason in my head. But I can tell other people don’t see the path, and they start looking lost.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being put into your mind by someone else, or that your thoughts are being taken out of your mind or broadcast to others?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like… thoughts being put in or broadcast, no. But I do get convinced sometimes that certain customers are coming in to check on me—like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask too much about the new showroom, even though my husband says they’re just being friendly.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel that people are talking about you, watching you, or acting in a way that’s meant to send you a message, even if others don’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Sometimes, yes. I know it sounds… a little odd, but I get convinced certain customers are coming in to check on me, like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask so many questions about the new showroom. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you—like things on TV, in the news, or conversations—were directed at you or had a special meaning just for you?\n\nPatient: Not TV or the news, really. It’s more… at the shop. Some customers ask too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re checking whether I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that simple to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear?\n\nPatient: Sometimes. When the shop is really quiet, I’ve heard… like a woman muttering my name from the polishing room. I’ll go check, and no one’s there. And once in a while I smell burning metal even when the torches are off. It feels real at the time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear, even when you’re not in the shop or in quiet places?\n\nPatient: Mostly it’s at the shop, when it’s quiet. I can’t really think of it happening much outside of there. Like I said, I’ll hear a woman mutter my name from the polishing room, or smell burning metal when the torches are off. It feels real enough that I go check.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel like your thoughts are completely out of order, or that nothing makes sense in the way it used to?\n\nPatient: Yes… sometimes. That’s part of why I came in. Someone will ask me something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and I’ll start answering but then I’m talking about invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. I can see their face change, like they’re not following me… and I’m not really following myself either.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or being broadcast so that others can hear them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about thoughts being put in or broadcast, exactly. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or that they are being broadcast so others can hear them?\n\nPatient: Not really like someone putting thoughts in my head or hearing them out loud. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but… I don’t know. It doesn’t feel that way.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel like your thoughts are completely out of order or that you can’t make sense of your own ideas, even when you're not talking to anyone else?\n\nPatient: Yes… even when I’m by myself, it can feel tangled. Like I’ll be thinking about something simple at the shop, a ring repair maybe, and then suddenly I’m on invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. It’s hard to make the thread come back.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts or beliefs that you know other people don’t understand, even if you try to explain them, like someone is secretly monitoring you or that you have a special role that no one else recognizes?\n\nPatient: I guess… yes, a little. It’s embarrassing.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about how your thoughts move from one idea to another when you're trying to explain something? Do they feel connected or do they jump around in a way that's hard to follow?\n\nPatient: They feel connected when I’m saying it… at least at first. But then I’ll answer something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and somehow I’m talking about invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. I can see people’s faces change, like they’re losing track of me. I guess it jumps around more than I realize.\n\nDoctor: When you're trying to explain something, do you ever feel like your thoughts are slipping away or that you can't organize your ideas the way you used to?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. It’s like I start with the answer, but then it slips sideways. Someone asks about a ring repair, and I’m suddenly on invoices, or soil for the garden, or mortgage papers. I don’t mean to… I just lose the shape of what I was trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel that your thoughts don’t follow a clear path or that you start talking about one thing and end up somewhere completely different without meaning to?\n\nPatient: Yes… that happens. I’ll start answering something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and then I’m talking about invoices, or garden soil, or the mortgage papers. I don’t really mean to go there. I just notice people kind of… stop following me.\n\nDoctor: When you're speaking and your thoughts seem to go in different directions, do your words and sentences still make sense to you, even if others might not follow them?\n\nPatient: To me they do, mostly. Like, I know why invoices connect to the repair, and then somehow garden soil or the mortgage papers come in… it feels like there’s a reason in my head. But I can tell other people don’t see the path, and they start looking lost.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being put into your mind by someone else, or that your thoughts are being taken out of your mind or broadcast to others?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like… thoughts being put in or broadcast, no. But I do get convinced sometimes that certain customers are coming in to check on me—like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask too much about the new showroom, even though my husband says they’re just being friendly.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel that people are talking about you, watching you, or acting in a way that’s meant to send you a message, even if others don’t see it that way?\n\nPatient: Sometimes, yes. I know it sounds… a little odd, but I get convinced certain customers are coming in to check on me, like whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask so many questions about the new showroom. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that things happening around you—like things on TV, in the news, or conversations—were directed at you or had a special meaning just for you?\n\nPatient: Not TV or the news, really. It’s more… at the shop. Some customers ask too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re checking whether I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that simple to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear?\n\nPatient: Sometimes. When the shop is really quiet, I’ve heard… like a woman muttering my name from the polishing room. I’ll go check, and no one’s there. And once in a while I smell burning metal even when the torches are off. It feels real at the time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that others don’t seem to hear, even when you’re not in the shop or in quiet places?\n\nPatient: Mostly it’s at the shop, when it’s quiet. I can’t really think of it happening much outside of there. Like I said, I’ll hear a woman mutter my name from the polishing room, or smell burning metal when the torches are off. It feels real enough that I go check.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel like your thoughts are completely out of order, or that nothing makes sense in the way it used to?\n\nPatient: Yes… sometimes. That’s part of why I came in. Someone will ask me something simple, like when a ring repair will be ready, and I’ll start answering but then I’m talking about invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. I can see their face change, like they’re not following me… and I’m not really following myself either.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or being broadcast so that others can hear them?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about thoughts being put in or broadcast, exactly. But… I have gotten pretty sure some customers are coming in just to see if I’m going to miss payments. They ask a lot about the new showroom, too many questions. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are being inserted into your mind by someone else, removed from your mind, or that they are being broadcast so others can hear them?\n\nPatient: Not really like someone putting thoughts in my head or hearing them out loud. It’s more… I get convinced people are watching for things. Like some customers asking too much about the new showroom, and I start thinking they’re really there to see if I’m going to miss payments. My husband says they’re just being friendly, but… I don’t know. It doesn’t feel that way.\n\nDoctor: Can you sometimes feel like your thoughts are completely out of order or that you can’t make sense of your own ideas, even when you're not talking to anyone else?\n\nPatient: Yes… even when I’m by myself, it can feel tangled. Like I’ll be thinking about something simple at the shop, a ring repair maybe, and then suddenly I’m on invoices, then garden soil, then mortgage papers. It’s hard to make the thread come back.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had thoughts or beliefs that you know other people don’t understand, even if you try to explain them, like someone is secretly monitoring you or that you have a special role that no one else recognizes?\n\nPatient: I guess… yes, a little. I know it sounds odd. I’ve gotten pretty convinced some customers come in just to check whether I’m going to miss payments. They ask too many questions about the new showroom. My husband says they’re only being friendly, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Can you describe if and how your thinking is affected when you're alone and not under pressure—do your thoughts still feel tangled or out of order then?\n\nPatient: Yes… it can still happen when I’m alone. Not always, but enough that I notice it.\n\nLike, if someone asks me something simple at work—when a ring repair will be ready—I’ll start answering and then my mind jumps to invoices, then garden soil, then the mortgage papers. And I can see their face change, like I’ve lost the point. Even when it’s quiet, my thoughts can still go that way.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
